# STATUS - what is running, what is next

One board for every phased plan. **Updated by the session that finishes a phase or review, in
the same commit as its Done note** (the x2-phase skill's Finish list). If a row and `git log`
disagree, `git log` wins; fix the row.

**How to start anything below:** open a new conversation, pick the model and effort in the row,
type `/x2-phase <plan> <phase>` (or `... review`). `/x2-phase status` answers "what's next" and
"what can run together" from this file.

**Running two sessions at once** is fine when (a) neither depends on the other, (b) at most one
of them edits code, or their "Touches" do not share a file, and (c) both commit with explicit
paths. Builds queue through one lock. Read-only steps (RE, reviews
that find nothing, planning) can always run alongside.

**One branch: `main` (user, 2026-10-16).** Every plan, phase and review works and commits on `main` in this Trunk. Create no new
branch and no worktree, and merge nothing. The old `mods/*` branches are history. **Never deploy from them** (june-item-kim K2 `f1549a6`):
the live `data036.kom` ships June's 228-byte `item.kim`, and an exe whose `Always.h` lacks `SERV_IRUHADEV_JUNE_ITEM_KIM` and
`SERV_IRUHADEV_KIM_LAYOUT_CHECK` reads it at 188 bytes per record **silently** (wrong items everywhere, no error). `main` has K2.

States: `done` · `review` (done, review pending) · `play-test` (waiting on the user) ·
`ready` (can start) · `queued` (could start, but the user ordered another plan first) ·
`blocked` (dependency not done) · `decision` (waiting on a user decision).

---

Parallel pairs with no shared file: none open now. 9a and 11 ran together on 2026-10-05, but both edited `X2GUUser.cpp` and `Always.h`, so each staged only its own hunks; 9b and 11 must not run together. (10 turned out to edit X2GameUnit/X2GUUser/X2GUNPC, so 9 + 10b was not a pair either.) Phases 10 and 11 both stage Lua
into `data036` and its `_manifest.json`, so run them one after the other. 15 and 11 both edit
`X2Lib/Offline/`. Phases 6b, 7, 7b, 7d, 8, 9, 15 and 16 all edit `X2GUUser.cpp` and/or `X2GameUnit.cpp`,
so run them one at a time; of these only 9 is known to be clear of 10's and 11's files.
Every code phase appends to `KTDXLIB/Always.h` (bytepatch re-reads it, so two appends in a row
are safe; two simultaneous ones are not).

## offline-grade - offline monster grade (real-server ranking)

Plan `OFFLINE_MONSTER_GRADE_PHASES.md` · branch `main` · log tag `[OFFLINE_GRADE]`
(offline_server.log) · flag `SERV_IRUHADEV_OFFLINE_MONSTER_GRADE` (the one flag; `_DEBUG` only for
short-lived lines) · kickoffs `kickoffs/offline-grade/` · created 2026-10-06 (user: "the proper
way"; socket test follows June, rewards ported in their own phase).

The emulator sends each NPC's monster grade as the real servers did. On dungeons that is the stage
script's `MONSTER_GRADE` (XSLDungeon), carried client-side to the NPC as June does. On battlefields
it is the grade per spawn path (XSLBattleField). Then the grade-keyed rewards. The socket boss terms
(june-buff 16-step3b2) then apply to dungeon grade-1 bosses that have an ExtraNPCStat row
(JUNE_BUGS #52 goes live with them).

## june-item-kim - June item.kim adoption

Plan `JUNE_ITEM_KIM_PHASES.md` · branch `main` · log tag `[JUNE_KIM]` · flags
`SERV_IRUHADEV_JUNE_ITEM_KIM` (record layout) + `SERV_IRUHADEV_KIM_LAYOUT_CHECK` (structural check) · kickoffs
`kickoffs/june-item-kim/` · backlog Stage 13 · created 2026-10-07.

March loads June's `item.kim` (111,428 items, 6,740 sets). June's records are 228 B against March's 188, and both files carry the same
header version (`0x1201`), so March would misread June's file silently. Every March id is in June. The plan ports June's record
verbatim, adds a layout check, and swaps the file.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## june-asset-extract - June non-lua assets, byte-exact, in March koms

Plan `JUNE_ASSET_EXTRACT_PHASES.md` · branch `main` · log tag `[KOM_LONG]` · flag
`SERV_IRUHADEV_KOM_LONG_NAMES` · kickoffs `kickoffs/june-asset-extract/` · backlog Stage 14 · created 2026-10-07.

**The answer.** June's non-lua/kim asset formats load in March unchanged. What breaks is the extraction: elstools cuts 18,571 June
V.0.4 entries short, about 4,900 of them damaged, and `data101_kom` is corrupt. Three packing rules also apply: manifest algorithm
values, cross-kom duplicates, and names over 59 characters. This plan builds a byte-exact extractor, a packing helper and a long-name
fix.

"Lanox lessons" compiled-Lua gate and A5's `DLG_Destination` line keep the old wording and are frozen. Use instead: `luasrc.py adopt-june` (June's file, default = `ljconv` bytecode),
`adopt-june --source` after a `core --add` row when the phase must edit it (A5 owns the `DLG_Destination_byButton` row, A3/A7 the `DLG_Map_*` rows), `luasrc.py convert` for a March file,
then `/elstools pack` (the pack gate replaces the compiled-header check). No hand compile or decompile.
E0's `ELYSION_SOLESS` decompile-check (`elstools decompile-batch` + `ljd_jun2017.py --check`) becomes
`luasrc.py adopt-june ELYSION_SOLESS.lua` (June's `ljconv` bytecode; `--source` only if the port must edit it, after a `core --add` row).

**june-effect C3y (2026-10-11):** the A8-A10 / E8-E11 dungeons' ids, worlds (enum / WorldData / files) and unresolved NPCs are rows of
`june2017_re/c3y_unreached_dungeon_census.tsv` / `c3y_unreached_world_census.tsv`. Their Hero rows go to june-dungeons C3y-5, not "out of scope"
(only the Mode Button is unreachable in June). Henir Space stages 32-35 already name the 4 boss worlds (`WI_ATLASDEVELOPMENT_BOSSATGE`,
`WI_TOWER_BOSSATGE`, `WI_SKYCROSSROADS_BOSSSTAGE`, `WI_ADRIAN_BOSSSTAGE`); **user, 2026-10-11: june-dungeons C3y-1b ports those 4 worlds**
(enum, WorldData, world + line map, assets). A8 / A10 / E8 / E9 reuse them and do not port them again.

## lua-source - core client Lua as proven plaintext source, the rest as June's bytecode

Plan `LUA_SOURCE_PHASES.md` · branch `main` · log tag `[LUA_SRC]` · flag `SERV_IRUHADEV_LUA_SOURCE` (+ `_DEBUG`, L6
only) · kickoffs `kickoffs/lua-source/` · skills `x2-lua`, `x2-adopt-june` (built here) · created 2026-10-09 (user request).

**The point.** The engine already loads XOR-encrypted BOM plaintext Lua next to bytecode (binder gate `LuaCommon/KLuabinder.h:24-61`,
parser linked), so there is no VM migration. This plan builds tools only:
- an instruction-level equivalence checker;
- `adopt-june`: every June `.lua` arrives as proven source, or as an exact `ljconv` fallback with a reason;
- `convert`: a March file becomes proven source only when a port must edit it;
- a pack gate that refuses anything else;
- Lua errors with line numbers in ErrorLog.

March files are not mass-converted, because June replaces them.

**Rescope (user, 2026-10-09, after L2).** Source only where a file must differ from June: the kept-merged files and files a port must
edit (the core list). Everything else ships as June's own bytecode transcoded by `ljconv` (header `0x82` -> `0x81`), as 3,638 files
already did on 2026-10-09. L4 dropped; L5 limited to core-list files.

## dev-overlay - loose Lua for development, pack at plan close-out and release

Plan `DEV_OVERLAY_PHASES.md` · branch `main` · log tag `[DEV_OVERLAY]` (`data\dev_overlay.log`) · flag
`SERV_IRUHADEV_DEV_OVERLAY` · kickoffs `kickoffs/dev-overlay/` · tool `tools/overlay.py` + skill `x2-overlay` (built here) ·
created 2026-10-09 (user request) · **`.lua` only** (user, 2026-10-09). Non-Lua assets keep the usual kom flow.

**The point.** The engine already reads loose files ahead of the koms (proven 2026-10-09 with a loose BOM-source dialog Lua).
- `.lua` development moves to `...\24965799\dev_overlay\<kom>\`. The engine indexes it at startup and serves it first, from
  `LoadRealFile` only.
- A per-kom `_overlay.json` records what changed.
- Lua is packed only at a plan's close-out and at release ("promote"), with decode-back byte equality as the proof.

Until O5 lands, the old Lua pack/copy flow stays in force for every other plan.
